{"step_index":3467,"source":"MODEL","type":"PLANNER_RESPONSE","status":"DONE","created_at":"2026-05-16T02:07:38Z","content":"Pushed (`12f4b87`). Here's the fix:\n\n### Problem\nThe Prastuta chart used a **UTC round-trip** (`now → UTC → add timezone → calculate`), while the InputScreen passes **local time directly**. The round-trip through integer UTC components introduced floating-point precision loss, causing a slight Lagna drift.\n\n### Fix — Both now use identical method:\n```dart\n// InputScreen (birth chart):\nlocalHour = h24 + _minute / 60.0;\n\n// Prastuta (now matches):\nlocalHour = now.hour + now.minute / 60.0 + now.second / 3600.0;\n```\n\nBoth pass `localHour` + `hourUtcOffset` directly to the calculator, which internally does `hour24 - hourUtcOffset` to get UTC. No round-trip, no precision loss. The only difference is Prastuta includes seconds for real-time accuracy."}
